FILE /round/ops/bound_digest.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """The round's ONE bound product-digest definition, plus the rejected alternative.
3: 
4: Independent challenge C8 (2026-10-10) reported `f2b288ad... (283 files)` from this round's
5: `product_snapshot.walk` scope while the AO brief published `4a039259...` from `equivalence.py`'s
6: narrower prefix scope. Both numbers were the round's own; the ambiguity was the round's defect.
7: 
8: This tool fixes the definition: it is `product_snapshot.walk` (PRODUCT_ROOTS, EXCLUDE_DIRS,
9: EXCLUDE_SUFFIX), rows = [rel_posix, sha256_file] sorted, digest = sha256 of canonical JSON of the
10: rows. It emits the bound digest for a candidate root together with the rejected alternative, and
11: states verbatim what a checker must reproduce.
12: """
13: 
14: from __future__ import annotations
15: 
16: import hashlib
17: import json
18: import sys
19: from pathlib import Path
20: 
21: EXCLUDE_DIRS = {".git", "__pycache__", ".hgk", ".agents", ".hermes", ".venv", "node_modules"}
22: EXCLUDE_SUFFIX = {".pyc", ".pyo"}
23: PRODUCT_ROOTS = ["src", "tests", "tools", "schemas", "dist", "docs", "skills", "fixtures",
24:                  "pyproject.toml", "README.md", "ACCEPTANCE.md", "LICENSE", "PROVENANCE.md",
25:                  "SBOM.cdx.json", "OWNER_LICENSE_DECISION.yaml", "openspec"]
26: # The definition `equivalence.py` used for the subject-binding line in the AO brief. REJECTED as the
27: # bound digest (it silently omits packaging metadata, LICENSE, openspec and other roots).
28: ALT_ROOTS = ["src", "tools", "tests", "schemas", "skills", "docs", "dist", "fixtures"]
29: 
30: BOUND_DEFINITION = ("product_digest = sha256 of canonical JSON (separators ',',':', ensure_ascii=False, "
31:                     "keys n/a) of the sorted rows [[posix_relative_path, sha256_file], ...] over "
32:                     "PRODUCT_ROOTS=" + json.dumps(PRODUCT_ROOTS) + ", excluding any path with a part in "
33:                     "EXCLUDE_DIRS=" + json.dumps(sorted(EXCLUDE_DIRS)) + " or a suffix in "
34:                     "EXCLUDE_SUFFIX=" + json.dumps(sorted(EXCLUDE_SUFFIX)) + ".")
35: 
36: 
37: def sha256_file(p: Path) -> str:
38:     h = hashlib.sha256()
39:     with p.open("rb") as fh:
40:         for b in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
41:             h.update(b)
42:     return h.hexdigest()
43: 
44: 
45: def walk(root: Path, roots: list[str]) -> list[Path]:
46:     out: list[Path] = []
47:     for rel in roots:
48:         t = root / rel
49:         if t.is_file():
50:             out.append(t)
51:         elif t.is_dir():
52:             for f in sorted(t.rglob("*")):
53:                 if not f.is_file():
54:                     continue
55:                 if any(part in EXCLUDE_DIRS for part in f.parts):
56:                     continue
57:                 if f.suffix in EXCLUDE_SUFFIX:
58:                     continue
59:                 out.append(f)
60:     return out
61: 
62: 
63: def digest(root: Path, roots=None) -> dict:
64:     files = walk(root, roots or PRODUCT_ROOTS)
65:     rows = [[str(f.relative_to(root)).replace("\\", "/"), sha256_file(f)] for f in files]
66:     rows.sort()
67:     return {"file_count": len(rows),
68:             "digest": hashlib.sha256(
69:                 json.dumps(rows, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest(),
70:             "roots": roots or PRODUCT_ROOTS}
71: 
72: 
73: if __name__ == "__main__":
74:     root = Path(sys.argv[1])
75:     out_path = Path(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None
76:     bound = digest(root)
77:     alt = digest(root, ALT_ROOTS)
78:     receipt = {
79:         "schema": "PIPD-R5P-DIGEST-BINDING/1",
80:         "candidate_root": str(root).replace("\\", "/"),
81:         "bound_definition": BOUND_DEFINITION,
82:         "bound_product_digest": bound["digest"],
83:         "bound_file_count": bound["file_count"],
84:         "rejected_alternative": {
85:             "definition": "equivalence.py product digest over PRODUCT_PREFIXES=" + json.dumps(ALT_ROOTS),
86:             "product_digest": alt["digest"], "file_count": alt["file_count"],
87:             "why_rejected": ("narrower scope: omits packaging/legal/openspec roots, so two of the round's "
88:                              "own scripts published different numbers for the same subject (challenge C8)")},
89:         "checker_reproduction": ("python ops/bound_digest.py <pristine_root> then compare "
90:                                  "bound_product_digest"),
91:     }
92:     print(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
93:     if out_path:
94:         out_path.write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")

codex
C7 的結果受平台及執行目錄影響：在 Linux 從 `/w` 執行原命令，仍得到退出碼 0、CANDIDATE，指定的 in-repo test 也失敗；從 `/ao` 執行同一 probe 則會拒絕。原因是 `C:/...` 在 Linux 仍由 `Path.resolve()` 當相對路徑處理。我會依實際路徑邊界與測試預期，判定這是產品缺陷還是 oracle 的平台假設。C8 已得到 **288 檔、4ee712e8…**，與 binding 檔一致。

26: class TestToolingExecutes(unittest.TestCase):
27:     def test_every_module_compiles(self) -> None:
28:         bad: list[str] = []
29:         for d in (ROOT / "src", ROOT / "tools", ROOT / "tests"):
30:             for f in sorted(d.rglob("*.py")):
31:                 try:
32:                     py_compile.compile(str(f), doraise=True, cfile=str(f.with_suffix(".pyc.tmp")))
33:                 except py_compile.PyCompileError as exc:
34:                     bad.append(f"{f.relative_to(ROOT)}: {exc}")
35:                 finally:
36:                     f.with_suffix(".pyc.tmp").unlink(missing_ok=True)
37:         self.assertEqual(bad, [], "modules failed to compile")
38: 
39:     def test_cli_smoke_runs(self) -> None:
40:         """The 13-command CLI surface must actually execute, not just import.
41: 
42:         Run inside a throwaway copy of the repo: the runner writes .hgk/artifacts/cli/CLI_SMOKE.json
43:         by design, and a test that rewrites tracked evidence makes "tree clean after a test run"
44:         impossible. The copy has every artifact the runner reads, so nothing is skipped.
45:         """
46:         import os as _os
47:         import shutil as _sh
48:         import tempfile as _tf
49:         with _tf.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
50:             dst = Path(tmp) / "repo"
51:             _sh.copytree(ROOT, dst, ignore=_sh.ignore_patterns(".git", "__pycache__", "*.db", "*.pyc"))
52:             r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "tools/cli_smoke.py"], cwd=str(dst),
53:                                capture_output=True, text=True,
54:                                env={**_os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"})
55:         self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0,
56:                          f"cli_smoke.py exited {r.returncode}\n{(r.stderr or r.stdout)[-800:]}")
57: 
58:     def test_no_read_text_receives_newline(self) -> None:
59:         """Pin the specific defect: Path.read_text takes no `newline` keyword."""
60:         offenders = []
61:         for d in (ROOT / "src", ROOT / "tools", ROOT / "tests"):
62:             for f in sorted(d.rglob("*.py")):
63:                 text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
64:                 i = 0
65:                 while True:
66:                     j = text.find(".read_text(", i)
67:                     if j < 0:
68:                         break
69:                     k = j + len(".read_text(")
70:                     depth, c = 1, k
71:                     while depth > 0 and c < len(text):
72:                         depth += (text[c] == "(") - (text[c] == ")")
73:                         c += 1
74:                     if "newline=" in text[k:c]:
75:                         offenders.append(str(f.relative_to(ROOT)))
76:                         break
77:                     i = c
78:         self.assertEqual(offenders, [], "read_text() was given a `newline` argument")
79: 
80: 
81:     def test_tracked_file_metric_excludes_vcs_internals(self) -> None:
82:         """Pin the oracle for `_tracked_file_count` in BOTH subjects it must serve (R2-E2).
83: 
84:         The shipped probe in `src/pipd_ls_sp/cli.py::_tracked_file_count` is the authoritative
85:         definition: it returns `git ls-files` when the subject IS a git checkout, and otherwise the
86:         count of files in the tree EXCLUDING `.git/` and `__pycache__/`. The old test asserted only
87:         the first branch against an unconditional `git ls-files` literal. That made the oracle
88:         subject-incorrect: in a non-git fresh copy the walk branch runs, git prints 0, and the test
89:         FAILED (R2-E2). The oracle is rebound to the probe's own defined behavior for the subject at
90:         hand, so it holds in both environments. It is not relaxed: each branch is pinned EXACTLY to
91:         the authoritative count (equality, not inequality), the walk branch is additionally pinned
92:         to exclude `.git/` internals and `__pycache__/` on a synthetic tree, and no magic constant
93:         is used.
94:         """
95:         import os as _os
96:         import shutil as _sh
97:         import subprocess as sp
98:         import tempfile as _tf
99:         from pipd_ls_sp.cli import _tracked_file_count
100: 
101:         def _walk_count(root: Path) -> int:
102:             # The probe's non-git branch, stated independently of the implementation.
103:             return sum(1 for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
104:                        and ".git" not in p.parts and "__pycache__" not in p.parts)
105: 
106:         def _git_tracked(root: Path, *, ceiling: str | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
107:             env = dict(_os.environ)
108:             if ceiling is not None:
109:                 env["GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES"] = ceiling
110:             r = sp.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "ls-files"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
111:             return r.returncode, len([l for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.strip()])
112: 
113:         # (a) On a real git checkout the probe must report EXACTLY the git-tracked count, never
114:         #     rglob (which would also sweep .git). This is the branch the old test covered.
115:         rc, tracked = _git_tracked(ROOT)
116:         if rc == 0:
117:             n = _tracked_file_count(ROOT)
118:             self.assertEqual(n, tracked,
119:                              "on a git checkout tracked_files must equal `git ls-files` exactly")
120:             self.assertLess(n, 1000, "tracked_files looks like it is counting .git internals again")
121: 
122:         # (b) A non-git copy (no .git at all) is the exact condition that broke the old oracle.
123:         #     GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES stops git from climbing to an enclosing repo, so the subject is
124:         #     genuinely non-git even when the temp dir sits inside a checkout; the ignore list drops
125:         #     any nested scratch so the copy cannot recurse into itself.
126:         with _tf.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
127:             dst = Path(td) / "PIPD"
128:             _sh.copytree(ROOT, dst,
129:                          ignore=_sh.ignore_patterns(".git", "__pycache__", "*.db"))
130:             self.assertFalse((dst / ".git").exists(), "the fresh copy must exclude .git")
131:             rc2, tracked2 = _git_tracked(dst, ceiling=str(Path(td).resolve()))
132:             self.assertNotEqual(rc2, 0, "the fresh copy must not resolve to a git checkout")

FILE test_tt_summary.py
1: """Guard the TT status projection (R3-EXT-04 / R-AUD-012).
2: 
3: The stored `.hgk/artifacts/TT_REGISTER.json` carried 22 rows in `tts[]` while `status_summary`
4: described only 17 and no `as_of_candidate` was bound to the head. `tools/tt_summary_check.py`
5: recomputes the projection from `tts[]` only; these tests pin that:
6: 
7:   1. after `--write`, the stored summary equals the recomputation (and binds the head);
8:   2. a status mutation makes the (previously fresh) stored summary fail `--assert` first;
9:   3. removing / adding a row fails `--assert` on `total`;
10:   4. load -> dump -> load preserves the row count, ids and statuses;
11:   5. the 22 rows are still present and no row's status was closed/changed by the tool.
12: 
13: Everything that mutates works on a COPY of the register in a temporary directory, so the tests
14: pass whether they run before or after the external `--write` acceptance step, and the real
15: register is never written by the suite.
16: """
17: from __future__ import annotations
18: 
19: import contextlib
20: import importlib.util
21: import io
22: import json
23: import shutil
24: import sys
25: import tempfile
26: import unittest
27: from collections import Counter
28: from pathlib import Path
29: 
30: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
31: sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
32: REGISTER = ROOT / ".hgk" / "artifacts" / "TT_REGISTER.json"
33: 
34: # The real histogram of the 22 rows. Pinning the exact multiset is what proves the tool neither
35: # renamed nor dropped a status.
36: EXPECTED_STATUS_COUNTS = {
37:     "OPEN": 16,
38:     "OPEN_OWNER_GATE": 2,
39:     "PARTIAL_CLOSED": 1,
40:     "PARTIAL": 1,
41:     "CLOSED": 1,
42:     "TEMP_CLOSED": 1,
43: }
44: 
45: 
46: def _load_tool():
47:     spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("tt_summary_check",
48:                                                   ROOT / "tools" / "tt_summary_check.py")
49:     mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
50:     assert spec.loader is not None
51:     spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
52:     return mod
53: 
54: 
55: TT = _load_tool()
56: 
57: 
58: class TtSummaryProjection(unittest.TestCase):
59:     def setUp(self) -> None:
60:         self.tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="tt-register-"))
61:         self.addCleanup(shutil.rmtree, self.tmp, ignore_errors=True)
62:         self.register = self.tmp / "TT_REGISTER.json"
63:         shutil.copyfile(REGISTER, self.register)
64: 
65:     # -- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
66:     def _run(self, *args: str) -> "tuple[int, str]":
67:         buf = io.StringIO()
68:         with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf):
69:             rc = TT.main([*args, "--register", str(self.register)])
70:         return rc, buf.getvalue()
71: 
72:     def _load(self, path: "Path | None" = None):
73:         return TT.load_register(path or self.register)
74: 
75:     def _dump(self, doc, path: "Path | None" = None) -> None:
76:         (path or self.register).write_text(json.dumps(doc, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n",
77:                                            encoding="utf-8")
78: 
79:     def _head(self):
80:         return TT.resolve_head(ROOT)["head"]
81: 
82:     # -- 1. stored summary == recomputation (after --write) --------------------------------
83:     def test_stored_summary_equals_recomputation_after_write(self) -> None:
84:         rc, _ = self._run("--write")
85:         self.assertEqual(rc, 0, "--write must succeed where HEAD is resolvable")
86:         doc = self._load()
87:         head = self._head()
88:         self.assertIsNotNone(head, "this checkout must expose a resolvable HEAD")
89:         expected = TT.build_projection(doc, head)
90:         self.assertEqual(doc["status_summary"], expected,
91:                          "after --write the stored summary must equal the recomputation")
92:         self.assertEqual(doc["as_of_candidate"], head, "candidate must be bound to HEAD")
93:         self.assertEqual(doc["status_summary"]["count"], doc["status_summary"]["total"])
94:         self.assertEqual(doc["status_summary"]["total"], len(doc["tts"]))
95:         # the buckets partition the rows exactly (nothing dropped, nothing double-counted)

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """G-S3-WEB gate — FW-05 / R-AUD-003 / R-AUD-015, real semantic checks.
3: 
4: The Web denominator is EXACTLY the five PIPD semantic documents:
5:     PIPD_BOOTSTRAP.md, PIPD_CANONICAL_CORE.md, PIPD_ROUTER_PROFILES.md,
6:     PIPD_ARTIFACT_SCHEMAS.md, PIPD_EVAL_HANDOFF.md
7: each carrying its source objects (object id / schema / schema version), a source
8: hash, a CapabilityLoss record and a NACK record, with content derived from the
9: canonical PIPD objects and profiles — not free text.
10: 
11: The site UI five files (index.html, app.js, styles.css, manifest.webmanifest,
12: README.md) are OPTIONAL_SITE_UI: explicitly excluded from the Web denominator and
13: incapable of passing this gate (R-AUD-003: the old gate passed on exactly those
14: five files).
15: 
16: Modes:
17:   default    regenerate the projection from the canonical sources, then check.
18:   --strict   check the delivered tree exactly as-is (no regeneration) and refuse
19:              every mutation with a typed reason:
20:                MUT-WEB-WRONGSET     wrong web set (UI files as the pack, missing
21:                                     or extra documents)
22:                MUT-EMPTY-PAYLOAD    empty document / empty record list
23:                MUT-MISSING-FIELD    missing meta field / record field
24:                MUT-SCOPE-WIDENING   scope widened beyond `dist/web/**`
25:                MUT-STALE-SOURCE     artifact does not match the live sources
26:                MUT-NOT-DERIVED      free-text or tampered document content
27:                MUT-WEB-WRONGSET     OPTIONAL_SITE_UI leaked into the denominator
28: Exit code 0 only on PASS. S5/S6 are NOT authorised: nothing here claims a live
29: HGK/GENIE integration PASS (fixture/mock contracts only).
30: """
31: from __future__ import annotations
32: 
33: import argparse
34: import json
35: import subprocess
36: import sys
37: from pathlib import Path
38: 
39: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
40: sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
41: OUT_DEFAULT = ROOT / "dist" / "web"
42: RECEIPT_DEFAULT = ROOT / ".hgk" / "artifacts" / "s3" / "WEB_PACK.json"
43: 
44: 
45: def main() -> int:
46:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
47:     ap.add_argument("--strict", action="store_true",
48:                     help="audit the tree as-is; refuse every mutation with a typed reason")
49:     ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(OUT_DEFAULT), help="projection surface directory")
50:     ap.add_argument("--receipt", default=str(RECEIPT_DEFAULT), help="receipt JSON path")
51:     args = ap.parse_args()
52: 
53:     from pipd_ls_sp import projection
54: 
55:     out = Path(args.out)
56:     if not args.strict:
57:         projection.project_surfaces(ROOT, out)
58:     result = projection.check_web_surface(ROOT, out)
59:     result["mode"] = "STRICT_CHECK_ONLY" if args.strict else "REGENERATE_THEN_CHECK"
60:     result["candidate_head"] = subprocess.run(
61:         ["git", "-C", str(ROOT), "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True,
62:         text=True).stdout.strip()
63:     receipt = Path(args.receipt)
64:     receipt.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
65:     receipt.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8",
66:                        newline="")
67:     print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
68:     return 0 if result["verdict"] == "PASS" else 1
69: 
70:

72:     raise SystemExit(main())

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """S0/S1 — materialise the 22 source-designated Technology Admission rulings (PKG-00 §9).
3: 
4: The source terminalises a DESIGN disposition for CAND-01..CAND-22; it does NOT license-verify or
5: execute them. This tool therefore never invents a PASS and never inflates a denominator: it records
6: the source state for every required column and computes the per-row verdict from the artefact.
7: 
8: Audit repair (R-AUD-007 / MUT-TECH-PIN, WO-TS-req-pipd-r3-fw-08):
9: 
10: * every one of the 22 rows carries an exact source pin (repository + version/commit, or the literal
11:   ``UNAVAILABLE`` together with a machine-readable reason), a licence field (or an explicit
12:   unknown-licence disposition that BLOCKS activation), a TTL/security note, a fallback, an exit
13:   criterion, a permission scope, a provider-off behaviour, and a per-row disposition from exactly
14:   ``{ADOPT, REFERENCE, EVAL_ONLY, QUARANTINE, REJECT}``;
15: * a row is reported ``active``/``installed`` ONLY when its disposition is ``ADOPT`` AND it carries a
16:   verified pin AND a known (non-unknown) licence. A non-ADOPT disposition can NEVER be reported as
17:   active/installed;
18: * ``--report`` recomputes the per-row verdict from the artefact and prints the TRUE denominator per
19:   gate (an honest ``0/22`` for ``G-TECH-SOURCE-PIN`` stays ``0/22``), and exits non-zero when any
20:   row claims active without a verified pin or with an unknown licence.
21: 
22: Everything is parsed from the source clause, so the records cannot drift from the clause they cite.
23: Where the source legitimately returns a family of dispositions (``ADOPT_WITH_WRAPPER``,
24: ``ADOPT_WITH_TRANSLATION``), the design disposition is normalised to the canonical per-row
25: disposition ``ADOPT`` and the exact source string is preserved in ``upstream_disposition``.

489:                           "installation and NOT a licence determination; both are receiver-side.",
490:         "candidate_head": _candidate_head(),
491:     }
492:     consistency_pass = (n == 22 and schema_ok == n and neg_pass == n and roll_pass == n
493:                         and disp_pass == n and not refused)
494:     # ``verdict`` stays as an alias of the artefact-consistency verdict; ``overall_verdict`` carries
495:     # the combined hard-gate outcome that determines the exit code.
496:     payload["artefact_consistency_verdict"] = "PASS" if consistency_pass else "FAIL"
497:     payload["verdict"] = payload["artefact_consistency_verdict"]
498:     if not consistency_pass:
499:         payload["overall_verdict"] = "FAIL"
500:     return payload
501: 
502: 
503: def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
504:     global ARTIFACT
505:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Technology admission rulings (PKG-00 §9).")
506:     ap.add_argument("--report", action="store_true",
507:                     help="compute the per-row verdict from the artefact and print TRUE denominators")
508:     ap.add_argument("--artifact", metavar="PATH",
509:                     help="compute the report over the admissions in this artefact copy instead of "
510:                          "the source/repo artefact; output is written next to the copy and the repo "
511:                          "artefact is left untouched")
512:     args = ap.parse_args(argv)
513: 
514:     out_dir = OUT
515:     if args.artifact:
516:         ARTIFACT = Path(args.artifact)
517:         out_dir = ARTIFACT.parent

519:     payload = build_payload()

521:         json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8", newline="")
522: 
523:     report = {
524:         "records": payload["records"],
525:         "schema_valid": payload["schema_valid"],
526:         "gate_summary": payload["gate_summary"],
527:         "admissions": payload["admissions"],
528:         "reported_active": payload["reported_active"],
529:         "refused_active_claims": payload["refused_active_claims"],
530:         "artefact_consistency_verdict": payload["artefact_consistency_verdict"],
531:         "hard_gate_verdicts": payload["hard_gate_verdicts"],
532:         "source_pin_summary": payload["source_pin_summary"],
533:         "consumer_guard": payload["consumer_guard"],
534:         "overall_verdict": payload["overall_verdict"],
535:         "verdict": payload["verdict"],
536:         # R-AUD-007 close criterion: the consistency verdict must never be readable as an overall
537:         # PASS over a hard gate that is honestly failing. The pin gate is surfaced separately and
538:         # its scope is stated, so an "overall PASS" can no longer mask "G-TECH-SOURCE-PIN 0/22".
539:         "verdict_scope": (
540:             "ARTEFACT_CONSISTENCY_ONLY: 22/22 rows schema-valid, MUT-TECH-PIN refused, rollback "
541:             "target present, dispositions in the closed set, no non-ADOPT row reported active. "
542:             "It does NOT assert G-TECH-SOURCE-PIN is satisfied."
543:         ),
544:         "verdict_alias": "verdict == artefact_consistency_verdict (kept for back-compat)",
545:         "hard_gate_verdict": {
546:             "G-TECH-SOURCE-PIN": {
547:                 "reported": payload["gate_summary"]["G-TECH-SOURCE-PIN"],
548:                 "verdict": ("FAIL"
549:                             if payload["gate_summary"]["G-TECH-SOURCE-PIN"].startswith("0/")
550:                             else "PASS"),
551:                 "covered_by_consistency_verdict": False,
552:             }
553:         },
554:     }
555:     print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
556: 
557:     # Non-zero whenever the HARD-GATE overall verdict is FAIL — a nested FAIL must never be buried
558:     # under a cosmetically-PASS consistency verdict.
559:     return 0 if payload["overall_verdict"] == "PASS" else 1
560:

563:     raise SystemExit(main())

85:     """
86:     if not goal or not goal.strip():
87:         raise IntakeInvalid("empty goal")
88:     sources = list(sources or [])
89:     if not sources:
90:         raise AuthorityUnknown("intake requires at least one source locator")
91:     for s in sources:
92:         if not Path(s).exists():
93:             raise AuthorityUnknown(f"source locator does not exist: {s}")
94:     payload = {"goal": goal.strip(), "sources": sources,
95:                "constraints": list(constraints or []), "non_goals": list(non_goals or [])}
96:     card = dict(payload)
97:     card["subject_id"] = content_id("INTENT", payload)
98:     card["content_hash"] = sha256_text(canonical_json(payload))
99:     card["schema_version"] = "IntentCard@1"
100:     card["atoms"] = req_compile.compile_requirements(goal.strip(), sources)
101:     return card
102: 
103: 
104: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- profile binding
105: def profile_record(profile_name: str, axes: list[str]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
106:     """Return (ProfileBinding record, full profile computation); the record is schema-exact."""
107:     full = compute_profile(profile_name, axes=axes)
108:     rec = _rec("ProfileBinding", "PB", {"p": full["profile"], "a": sorted(axes)},
109:                profile=full["profile"], axes=sorted(set(axes)),
110:                vetoes=full["vetoes"], artifact_depth=full["artifact_depth"],
111:                assurance=full["assurance"])
112:     return rec, full
113: 
114: 
115: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- PI
116: def compile_pi(card: dict[str, Any], profile_name: str = "LITE") -> dict[str, Any]:
117:     """Compile the clause-bound atoms of an IntentCard into a PI package.
118: 
119:     The card's `atoms` field is a CLAIM, not an authority: the atom set is re-derived from the
120:     card's source clauses. A clause-bound claim must match the re-derivation exactly (tamper /
121:     collapse detection, refused otherwise); a legacy five-axis keyword claim is ignored outright -
122:     that shape is no longer an authoritative route into a RequirementAtom record.
123:     """
124:     compiled = req_compile.compile_requirements(str(card.get("goal") or "").strip(),
125:                                                 list(card.get("sources") or []))
126:     claimed = list(card.get("atoms") or [])
127:     if claimed:
128:         try:
129:             req_compile.validate_atoms(claimed)
130:         except PiSemanticFail:
131:             claimed = []  # legacy keyword shape: an unverified claim, never an authority
132:         if claimed and claimed != compiled:
133:             raise PiSemanticFail("semantic collapse: claimed atoms diverge from their source clauses")
134:     src_atoms = compiled
135:     axes = sorted({a.get("axis") or "intent" for a in src_atoms})
136:     pb, profile_meta = profile_record(profile_name, axes)
137:     atoms = []
138:     for a in src_atoms:
139:         atoms.append(_rec("RequirementAtom", "ATOM",
140:                           {"req": a["req_id"], "goal": card["content_hash"]},
141:                           req_id=a["req_id"],
142:                           source_clause=req_compile.normalized_locator(a["source_clause"]),
143:                           owner=a.get("owner", "PIPD-EC"),
144:                           acceptance_cue=a["acceptance_cue"],
145:                           risk_guard=a["risk_guard"]))
146:     if not atoms:
147:         raise PiSemanticFail("PI compiled zero atoms")
148:     payload = {"intent": card["subject_id"], "atoms": [a["req_id"] for a in atoms],
149:                "profile": pb["profile"]}
150:     pi = _rec("PI-PKG", "PI", payload)
151:     pi["stable_semantic_contract"] = {"contract": "stable", "profile_binding": pb,
152:                                       "atoms": atoms, "goal": card["goal"],
153:                                       # normalized source table: one entry per source clause, so no
154:                                       # atom has to embed a full copy of its clause (payload economy)
155:                                       "sources": req_compile.source_table(src_atoms)}
156:     pi["acceptance"] = {"mode": "bound", "oracle_source": "DOC-03 DOMAIN_ORACLES"}
157:     _seal(pi, "PI")
158:     pi["trace"] = [_rec("TraceLink", "TL", {"f": pi["subject_id"], "t": a["subject_id"]},
159:                         from_type="PI-PKG", from_id=pi["subject_id"], from_hash=pi["content_hash"],
160:                         to_type="RequirementAtom", to_id=a["subject_id"],
161:                         to_hash=a["content_hash"],
162:                         rationale="PI derives this atom from the frozen intent")
163:                    for a in atoms]
164:     pi["_profile_meta"] = profile_meta  # non-record sidecar, stripped before validation
165:     return pi
166: 
167: 
168: def _seal(rec: dict[str, Any], prefix: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
169:     """Recompute identity over the FINAL body.
170: 
171:     Builders that add declared fields after _rec() must seal at the end, otherwise the hash does
172:     not cover the body and validate.semantic_invariants correctly rejects the record.
173:     """
174:     # `trace` is a derived relation, not body content: it carries the parent hash, so it cannot be
175:     # inside the hash it references. The validator excludes exactly the same key set.
176:     body = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("subject_id", "version", "content_hash", "schema_version", "trace", "_profile_meta")}
177:     digest = sha256_text(canonical_json(body))
178:     rec["subject_id"] = f"{prefix}-{digest[:16]}"
179:     rec["content_hash"] = digest
180:     return rec

FILE /w/src/pipd_ls_sp/requirements.py
104: 
105: def _polarity(statement: str, negation_inherited: bool) -> str:
106:     if negation_inherited or NEGATION.search(statement):
107:         return 'MUST NOT'
108:     return 'MUST'
109: 
110: 
111: def compile_requirements(goal: str, sources: list[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
112:     """Compile source clauses into clause-bound atomic requirements.
113: 
114:     A directory source binds the submitted goal as its inline clause source; a file source is read
115:     as authoritative clause text. Normative claims without a source locator are refused.
116:     """
117:     if not sources:
118:         raise IntakeInvalid('normative claim without a source locator is refused')
119:     atoms: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
120:     for source in sources:
121:         path = Path(source)
122:         inline = path.is_dir()
123:         try:
124:             text = goal if inline else path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
125:         except (OSError, UnicodeError) as exc:
126:             raise IntakeInvalid(f'cannot read clause source: {path}') from exc
127:         for start, end, clause in _parts(text):
128:             clause_id = f'clause-{start}-{end}'
129:             for child, (statement, negation_inherited) in enumerate(_expand(clause)):
130:                 if _is_filler(statement):
131:                     continue
132:                 polarity = _polarity(statement, negation_inherited)
133:                 locator = {'file': str(path), 'kind': 'inline-goal' if inline else 'file',
134:                            'clause_id': clause_id, 'span': [start, end], 'child': child,
135:                            'text': statement, 'source_text': clause,
136:                            'source_sha256': sha256_text(text)}
137:                 rid = 'REQ-' + sha256_text(canonical_json(locator))[:20]
138:                 atoms.append({'req_id': rid, 'source_clause': locator, 'owner': 'PIPD-EC',
139:                               'axis': advisory_axis(statement),
140:                               'acceptance_cue': f'{polarity}: {statement}',
141:                               'risk_guard': {
142:                                   'polarity': polarity,
143:                                   'oracle': {'id': 'ORACLE-' + rid, 'predicate': statement,
144:                                              'expected': 'forbidden' if polarity == 'MUST NOT'
145:                                              else 'required'},

255:     if head is None:
256:         print("WARNING: HEAD is unresolvable here; as_of_candidate cannot be bound. "
257:               "The summary comparison is still performed.")

261:         return 1

263:           f"unknown={expected['unknown']} blocking_count={expected['blocking_count']} "
